{"Write":"A:/muzz/backend/src/feed/feed.service.ts: import { BadRequestException, ConflictException, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { Db } from '../db/db';\nimport { FeedCard, UserRow } from '../db/types';\nimport { PushService } from '../notifications/push.service';\n\nexport interface FeedFilters {\n  minAge?: number;\n  maxAge?: number;\n  radiusKm?: number;\n  limit?: number;\n  offset?: number;\n}\n\nexport interface LikeOutcome {\n  liked: true;\n  matched: boolean;\n  matchId: string | null;\n  likesRemainingToday: number;\n}\n\n/**\n * Feed + interactions. The quota decrement and the candidate query are the two\n * pieces of logic the spec calls out as race- and correctness-sensitive, so they\n * are kept as literal SQL here rather than built up through the Repo helper.\n */\n@Injectable()\nexport class FeedService {\n  constructor(\n    private readonly db: Db,\n    private readonly push: PushService,\n  ) {}\n\n  async feed(user: UserRow, f: FeedFilters): Promise<FeedCard[]> {\n    const limit = Math.min(Math.max(f.limit ?? 20, 1), 50);\n    const offset = Math.max(f.offset ?? 0, 0);\n\n    return this.db.repo<FeedCard>('feed').all(\n      `WITH me AS (SELECT gender, lat, lng FROM users WHERE id = $1),\n            mine AS (SELECT interest_id FROM user_interests WHERE user_id = $1),\n            candidates AS (\n         SELECT u.id AS user_id, u.gender, u.location,\n                EXTRACT(YEAR FROM age(u.dob))::int AS age,\n                p.bio, p.profession, p.education, p.marital_status, p.photos,\n                p.verification_status,\n                (SELECT count(*)::int FROM user_interests x\n                  WHERE x.user_id = u.id\n                    AND x.interest_id IN (SELECT interest_id FROM mine)) AS shared_interests,\n                -- Haversine inline rather than the earthdistance extension: same\n                -- result, no extension to provision on a managed Postgres.\n                CASE\n                  WHEN u.lat IS NULL OR u.lng IS NULL OR $2::float8 IS NULL THEN NULL\n                  ELSE 6371 * acos(least(1, greatest(-1,\n                         sin(radians($2::float8)) * sin(radians(u.lat)) +\n                         cos(radians($2::float8)) * cos(radians(u.lat)) *\n                         cos(radians(u.lng - $3::float8)))))\n                END AS distance_km\n           FROM users u\n           JOIN profiles p ON p.user_id = u.id\n          WHERE u.id <> $1\n            AND u.gender IS NOT NULL\n            AND u.gender <> (SELECT gender FROM me)\n            -- Only verified profiles are browsable; re-verification hides a\n            -- profile again until it resolves (spec 2.3).\n            AND p.verification_status = 'verified'\n            AND ($4::int IS NULL OR EXTRACT(YEAR FROM age(u.dob)) >= $4)\n            AND ($5::int IS NULL OR EXTRACT(YEAR FROM age(u.dob)) <= $5)\n            -- Spec 2.3: exclude passes and blocks in both directions.\n            AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM passes  WHERE user_id     = $1 AND passed_user_id = u.id)\n            AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM blocks  WHERE blocker_id  = $1 AND blocked_id    = u.id)\n            AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM blocks  WHERE blocked_id  = $1 AND blocker_id    = u.id)\n            -- An outgoing like is a swipe-past too: never re-serve the card.\n            AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM likes   WHERE from_user_id = $1 AND to_user_id   = u.id)\n            AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM matches WHERE (user_a_id = $1 AND user_b_id = u.id)\n                                                     OR (user_b_id = $1 AND user_a_id = u.id))\n       )\n       SELECT * FROM candidates\n        -- No location on either side means we cannot filter, so we show rather\n        -- than silently empty the feed.\n        WHERE ($6::float8 IS NULL OR distance_km IS NULL OR distance_km <= $6::float8)\n        -- Spec 1.2: ranked by interest-tag overlap. Distance and recency break ties.\n        ORDER BY shared_interests DESC, distance_km ASC NULLS LAST, user_id\n        LIMIT $7 OFFSET $8`,\n      [user.id, user.lat, user.lng, f.minAge ?? null, f.maxAge ?? null, f.radiusKm ?? null, limit, offset],\n    );\n  }\n\n  /** Spec 2.3 — atomic quota check + decrement, then like and possible match. */\n  async like(user: UserRow, targetId: string): Promise<LikeOutcome> {\n    if (user.id === targetId) throw new BadRequestException('cannot_like_self');\n\n    const blocked = await this.db.count(\n      `SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM blocks\n        WHERE (blocker_id = $1 AND blocked_id = $2) OR (blocker_id = $2 AND blocked_id = $1)`,\n      [user.id, targetId],\n    );\n    if (blocked) throw new ConflictException('blocked');\n\n    const target = await this.db.one<{ id: string }>('SELECT id FROM users WHERE id = $1', [targetId]);\n    if (!target) throw new BadRequestException('unknown_user');\n\n    return this.db.tx(async (conn) => {\n      // Never read-then-write: the WHERE clause is the concurrency control, so a\n      // double-tap can only ever consume one like.\n      const decremented = await conn.query(\n        `UPDATE subscriptions\n            SET likes_remaining_today = likes_remaining_today - 1\n          WHERE user_id = $1\n            AND status = 'active'\n            AND likes_remaining_today > 0\n        RETURNING likes_remaining_today`,\n        [user.id],\n      );\n\n      if (!decremented.rowCount) {\n        // Zero rows is ambiguous, so ask why once the write has already failed.\n        const sub = await conn.query(\n          'SELECT status, likes_remaining_today FROM subscriptions WHERE user_id = $1',\n          [user.id],\n        );\n        const row = sub.rows[0] as { status: string; likes_remaining_today: number } | undefined;\n        if (!row || row.status !== 'active') throw new ConflictException('subscription_inactive');\n        throw new ConflictException('no_likes_remaining');\n      }\n\n      await conn.query(\n        `INSERT INTO likes (from_user_id, to_user_id) VALUES ($1, $2)\n         ON CONFLICT (from_user_id, to_user_id) DO NOTHING`,\n        [user.id, targetId],\n      );\n\n      const reciprocal = await conn.query(\n        'SELECT 1 FROM likes WHERE from_user_id = $2 AND to_user_id = $1',\n        [user.id, targetId],\n      );\n      if (!reciprocal.rowCount) {\n        const remaining = (decremented.rows[0] as { likes_remaining_today: number }).likes_remaining_today;\n        // Fire-and-forget outside the transaction would be better once volume\n        // justifies a queue; inline keeps it simple at MVP.\n        void this.push.toUser(targetId, 'new_like', { fromUserId: user.id });\n        return { liked: true, matched: false, matchId: null, likesRemainingToday: remaining };\n      }\n\n      // Pair is stored normalized so the UNIQUE index prevents a duplicate match.\n      const match = await conn.query(\n        `INSERT INTO matches (user_a_id, user_b_id)\n         VALUES (least($1::uuid, $2::uuid), greatest($1::uuid, $2::uuid))\n         ON CONFLICT (user_a_id, user_b_id) DO UPDATE SET unmatched_at = NULL\n         RETURNING id`,\n        [user.id, targetId],\n      );\n      const matchId = (match.rows[0] as { id: string }).id;\n\n      void this.push.toUser(targetId, 'new_match', { matchId });\n      void this.push.toUser(user.id, 'new_match', { matchId });\n\n      const remaining = (decremented.rows[0] as { likes_remaining_today: number }).likes_remaining_today;\n      return { liked: true, matched: true, matchId, likesRemainingToday: remaining };\n    });\n  }\n\n  async pass(user: UserRow, targetId: string): Promise<{ passed: true }> {\n    if (user.id === targetId) throw new BadRequestException('cannot_pass_self');\n    await this.db.query(\n      `INSERT INTO passes (user_id, passed_user_id) VALUES ($1, $2)\n       ON CONFLICT (user_id, passed_user_id) DO NOTHING`,\n      [user.id, targetId],\n    );\n    return { passed: true };\n  }\n\n  /** Spec 1.2 — block removes the pair from both feeds and ends any match. */\n  async block(user: UserRow, targetId: string, reason?: string): Promise<{ blocked: true }> {\n    if (user.id === targetId) throw new BadRequestException('cannot_block_self');\n    await this.db.tx(async (conn) => {\n      await conn.query(\n        `INSERT INTO blocks (blocker_id, blocked_id) VALUES ($1, $2)\n         ON CONFLICT (blocker_id, blocked_id) DO NOTHING`,\n        [user.id, targetId],\n      );\n      await conn.query(\n        `UPDATE matches SET unmatched_at = now()\n          WHERE unmatched_at IS NULL\n            AND ((user_a_id = $1 AND user_b_id = $2) OR (user_a_id = $2 AND user_b_id = $1))`,\n        [user.id, targetId],\n      );\n      if (reason) {\n        await conn.query(\n          'INSERT INTO reports (reporter_id, reported_id, reason) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)',\n          [user.id, targetId, reason],\n        );\n      }\n    });\n    return { blocked: true };\n  }\n}\n"}

{"Write":"A:/muzz/backend/src/notifications/push.service.ts: import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { Db } from '../db/db';\nimport { firebaseApp } from '../auth/auth';\n\nexport type PushCategory = 'new_like' | 'new_match' | 'new_message';\n\n/** Typed map, so the preference column is never built from caller input. */\nconst PREFERENCE_COLUMN: Record<PushCategory, string> = {\n  new_like: 'new_like',\n  new_match: 'new_match',\n  new_message: 'new_message',\n};\n\n@Injectable()\nexport class PushService {\n  private readonly log = new Logger(PushService.name);\n\n  constructor(private readonly db: Db) {}\n\n  /**\n   * Spec 1.2/2.1 — triggered server-side on like/match/message. Respects the\n   * recipient's per-category toggle and never throws: a push failure must not\n   * fail the like that caused it.\n   */\n  async toUser(userId: string, category: PushCategory, data: Record<string, string>): Promise<void> {\n    try {\n      const prefs = await this.db.one<Record<string, boolean>>(\n        'SELECT * FROM notification_preferences WHERE user_id = $1',\n        [userId],\n      );\n      const column = PREFERENCE_COLUMN[category];\n      // Absent row means \"never touched the screen\" -> defaults apply, which are on.\n      if (prefs && prefs[column] === false) return;\n\n      const tokens = await this.db.all<{ token: string }>(\n        'SELECT token FROM device_tokens WHERE user_id = $1',\n        [userId],\n      );\n      if (!tokens.length) return;\n\n      const { getMessaging } = await import('firebase-admin/messaging');\n      const res = await getMessaging(firebaseApp()).sendEachForMulticast({\n        tokens: tokens.map((t) => t.token),\n        data: { category, ...data },\n        android: { priority: 'high' },\n        apns: { payload: { aps: { contentAvailable: true } } },\n      });\n\n      await this.prune(res.responses, tokens.map((t) => t.token));\n    } catch (err) {\n      this.log.warn(`push to ${userId} failed: ${String(err)}`);\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** Drops tokens FCM reports as gone so the table does not grow forever. */\n  private async prune(\n    responses: { success: boolean; error?: { code?: string } }[],\n    tokens: string[],\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const dead = tokens.filter((_, i) => {\n      const code = responses[i]?.error?.code;\n      return (\n        code === 'messaging/registration-token-not-registered' ||\n        code === 'messaging/invalid-registration-token'\n      );\n    });\n    if (dead.length) await this.db.query('DELETE FROM device_tokens WHERE token = ANY($1)', [dead]);\n  }\n}\n"}
{"Write":"A:/muzz/backend/src/db/db.ts: import { Injectable, OnModuleDestroy } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { Pool, PoolClient } from 'pg';\n\n/** Anything that can run SQL — the Pool, or a client inside a transaction. */\nexport interface Queryable {\n  query(sql: string, params?: unknown[]): Promise<{ rows: any[]; rowCount: number | null }>;\n}\n\n/**\n * The read shapes every caller needs, over any Queryable. Shared by Db and Repo\n * so the SQL-running code exists once rather than as a copy on each.\n */\nabstract class Reader {\n  protected constructor(protected readonly db: Queryable) {}\n\n  async all<T>(sql: string, params: unknown[] = []): Promise<T[]> {\n    return (await this.db.query(sql, params)).rows as T[];\n  }\n\n  async one<T>(sql: string, params: unknown[] = []): Promise<T | null> {\n    return ((await this.db.query(sql, params)).rows[0] ?? null) as T | null;\n  }\n\n  async count(sql: string, params: unknown[] = []): Promise<number> {\n    const rows = (await this.db.query(sql, params)).rows as { n: number | string }[];\n    return Number(rows[0]?.n ?? 0);\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Generic table gateway. One class serves every table: the only thing that\n * varies is the table name, so it is a constructor argument rather than a\n * subclass per entity.\n *\n * Column names are interpolated into SQL. That is safe because they come from\n * the DTO's own keys in server-authored code — never from a request body.\n */\nexport class Repo<T> extends Reader {\n  constructor(db: Queryable, readonly table: string) {\n    super(db);\n  }\n\n  byId(id: string): Promise<T | null> {\n    return this.one<T>(`SELECT * FROM ${this.table} WHERE id = $1`, [id]);\n  }\n\n  /** INSERT built from the object's own keys — one path for every table. */\n  async insert(dto: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<T> {\n    const keys = Object.keys(dto);\n    const cols = keys.map((k) => `\"${k}\"`).join(', ');\n    const holes = keys.map((_, i) => `$${i + 1}`).join(', ');\n    const row = await this.one<T>(\n      `INSERT INTO ${this.table} (${cols}) VALUES (${holes}) RETURNING *`,\n      Object.values(dto),\n    );\n    return row as T;\n  }\n\n  /** UPDATE built from the object's own keys. */\n  async set(id: string, dto: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<T | null> {\n    const keys = Object.keys(dto);\n    if (!keys.length) return this.byId(id);\n    const assignments = keys.map((k, i) => `\"${k}\" = $${i + 2}`).join(', ');\n    return this.one<T>(\n      `UPDATE ${this.table} SET ${assignments} WHERE id = $1 RETURNING *`,\n      [id, ...Object.values(dto)],\n    );\n  }\n\n  async remove(id: string): Promise<number> {\n    return (await this.db.query(`DELETE FROM ${this.table} WHERE id = $1`, [id])).rowCount ?? 0;\n  }\n}\n\n@Injectable()\nexport class Db extends Reader implements Queryable, OnModuleDestroy {\n  /** ponytail: fixed pool of 10. Raise max if p99 latency tracks pool wait. */\n  private readonly pool = new Pool({\n    connectionString: process.env.DATABASE_URL,\n    max: 10,\n  });\n\n  constructor() {\n    super(null as unknown as Queryable);\n    // Reader holds the Queryable; Db is its own connection source.\n    (this as { db: Queryable }).db = this;\n  }\n\n  query(sql: string, params: unknown[] = []) {\n    return this.pool.query(sql, params);\n  }\n\n  /** Bound to a specific connection when inside a transaction. */\n  repo<T>(table: string, conn: Queryable = this): Repo<T> {\n    return new Repo<T>(conn, table);\n  }\n\n  /** Runs fn in a transaction, rolling back on any throw. */\n  async tx<R>(fn: (conn: Queryable) => Promise<R>): Promise<R> {\n    const client: PoolClient = await this.pool.connect();\n    try {\n      await client.query('BEGIN');\n      const out = await fn(client);\n      await client.query('COMMIT');\n      return out;\n    } catch (err) {\n      await client.query('ROLLBACK');\n      throw err;\n    } finally {\n      client.release();\n    }\n  }\n\n  onModuleDestroy() {\n    return this.pool.end();\n  }\n}\n"}
{"Write":"A:/muzz/backend/src/db/db.ts: import { Injectable, OnModuleDestroy } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { Pool, PoolClient } from 'pg';\n\nexport interface QueryResultLike {\n  rows: any[];\n  rowCount: number | null;\n}\n\n/** Anything that can run SQL — the Pool, or a client inside a transaction. */\nexport interface Queryable {\n  query(sql: string, params?: unknown[]): Promise<QueryResultLike>;\n}\n\n/**\n * The read shapes every caller needs. Defined once here and inherited by both\n * Db and Repo, so the SQL-running code is not duplicated across them.\n */\nabstract class Reader {\n  abstract query(sql: string, params?: unknown[]): Promise<QueryResultLike>;\n\n  async all<T>(sql: string, params: unknown[] = []): Promise<T[]> {\n    return (await this.query(sql, params)).rows as T[];\n  }\n\n  async one<T>(sql: string, params: unknown[] = []): Promise<T | null> {\n    return ((await this.query(sql, params)).rows[0] ?? null) as T | null;\n  }\n\n  async count(sql: string, params: unknown[] = []): Promise<number> {\n    const rows = (await this.query(sql, params)).rows as { n: number | string }[];\n    return Number(rows[0]?.n ?? 0);\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Generic table gateway. One class serves every table: the only thing that\n * varies is the table name, so it is a constructor argument rather than a\n * subclass per entity.\n *\n * Table and column names are interpolated into SQL. That is safe because they\n * come from server-authored code — never from a request body.\n */\nexport class Repo<T> extends Reader {\n  constructor(private readonly conn: Queryable, readonly table: string) {\n    super();\n  }\n\n  query(sql: string, params: unknown[] = []): Promise<QueryResultLike> {\n    return this.conn.query(sql, params);\n  }\n\n  byId(id: string): Promise<T | null> {\n    return this.one<T>(`SELECT * FROM ${this.table} WHERE id = $1`, [id]);\n  }\n\n  /** INSERT built from the object's own keys — one path for every table. */\n  async insert(dto: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<T> {\n    const keys = Object.keys(dto);\n    const cols = keys.map((k) => `\"${k}\"`).join(', ');\n    const holes = keys.map((_, i) => `$${i + 1}`).join(', ');\n    const row = await this.one<T>(\n      `INSERT INTO ${this.table} (${cols}) VALUES (${holes}) RETURNING *`,\n      Object.values(dto),\n    );\n    return row as T;\n  }\n\n  /** UPDATE built from the object's own keys. */\n  async set(id: string, dto: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<T | null> {\n    const keys = Object.keys(dto);\n    if (!keys.length) return this.byId(id);\n    const assignments = keys.map((k, i) => `\"${k}\" = $${i + 2}`).join(', ');\n    return this.one<T>(\n      `UPDATE ${this.table} SET ${assignments} WHERE id = $1 RETURNING *`,\n      [id, ...Object.values(dto)],\n    );\n  }\n\n  async remove(id: string): Promise<number> {\n    return (await this.query(`DELETE FROM ${this.table} WHERE id = $1`, [id])).rowCount ?? 0;\n  }\n}\n\n@Injectable()\nexport class Db extends Reader implements Queryable, OnModuleDestroy {\n  /** ponytail: fixed pool of 10. Raise max if p99 latency tracks pool wait. */\n  private readonly pool = new Pool({\n    connectionString: process.env.DATABASE_URL,\n    max: 10,\n  });\n\n  query(sql: string, params: unknown[] = []): Promise<QueryResultLike> {\n    return this.pool.query(sql, params);\n  }\n\n  /** A table gateway, bound to a transaction connection when one is supplied. */\n  repo<T>(table: string, conn: Queryable = this): Repo<T> {\n    return new Repo<T>(conn, table);\n  }\n\n  /** Runs fn in a transaction, rolling back on any throw. */\n  async tx<R>(fn: (conn: Queryable) => Promise<R>): Promise<R> {\n    const client: PoolClient = await this.pool.connect();\n    try {\n      await client.query('BEGIN');\n      const out = await fn(client);\n      await client.query('COMMIT');\n      return out;\n    } catch (err) {\n      await client.query('ROLLBACK');\n      throw err;\n    } finally {\n      client.release();\n    }\n  }\n\n  onModuleDestroy() {\n    return this.pool.end();\n  }\n}\n"}

{"Write":"A:/muzz/backend/src/subscription/subscription.service.ts: import { Injectable, Logger, UnauthorizedException } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { Cron, CronExpression } from '@nestjs/schedule';\nimport { Db } from '../db/db';\nimport { SubscriptionRow, SubscriptionStatus } from '../db/types';\n\n/** Product id -> plan. RevenueCat sends the product, not our plan name. */\nconst PLAN_BY_PRODUCT: Record<string, string> = {\n  rishta_plus_monthly: 'plus',\n  rishta_plus_yearly: 'plus',\n  rishta_gold_monthly: 'gold',\n  rishta_gold_yearly: 'gold',\n};\n\nconst ALLOWANCE_BY_PLAN: Record<string, number> = {\n  free: Number(process.env.FREE_PLAN_DAILY_LIKES ?? 10),\n  plus: 50,\n  gold: 999,\n};\n\ninterface WebhookEvent {\n  type: string;\n  app_user_id: string;\n  product_id?: string;\n  expiration_at_ms?: number;\n  period_type?: string;\n}\n\n@Injectable()\nexport class SubscriptionService {\n  private readonly log = new Logger(SubscriptionService.name);\n\n  constructor(private readonly db: Db) {}\n\n  /**\n   * Every user has exactly one subscription row, including free users, so the\n   * atomic decrement in FeedService.like is always a single UPDATE.\n   */\n  async ensure(userId: string): Promise<SubscriptionRow> {\n    const allowance = ALLOWANCE_BY_PLAN.free;\n    await this.db.query(\n      `INSERT INTO subscriptions (user_id, status, plan, daily_allowance, likes_remaining_today)\n       VALUES ($1, 'active', 'free', $2, $2)\n       ON CONFLICT (user_id) DO NOTHING`,\n      [userId, allowance],\n    );\n    return (await this.db.one<SubscriptionRow>('SELECT * FROM subscriptions WHERE user_id = $1', [\n      userId,\n    ])) as SubscriptionRow;\n  }\n\n  async status(userId: string) {\n    const sub = await this.ensure(userId);\n    return {\n      status: sub.status,\n      plan: sub.plan,\n      renewsAt: sub.renews_at,\n      startedAt: sub.started_at,\n      // Spec 1.2 — surfaced prominently so the client can disable Like at zero\n      // rather than letting the button silently fail.\n      likesRemainingToday: sub.likes_remaining_today,\n      dailyAllowance: sub.daily_allowance,\n      quotaResetAt: sub.quota_reset_at,\n      // Spec 1.2 — an inactive plan gates new likes only; chat stays open.\n      canSendLikes: sub.status === 'active' && sub.likes_remaining_today > 0,\n    };\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Spec 2.3 — quota resets on a schedule, never on login. Resetting at\n   * authentication would let anyone refill by logging out and back in.\n   */\n  @Cron(CronExpression.EVERY_DAY_AT_MIDNIGHT)\n  async nightlyQuotaReset(): Promise<void> {\n    const lapsed = await this.db.query(\n      `UPDATE subscriptions SET status = 'expired'\n        WHERE status = 'active' AND renews_at IS NOT NULL AND renews_at < now()`,\n    );\n    const reset = await this.db.query(\n      `UPDATE subscriptions\n          SET likes_remaining_today = daily_allowance, quota_reset_at = now()\n        WHERE status = 'active'`,\n    );\n    this.log.log(`quota reset: ${reset.rowCount ?? 0} active, ${lapsed.rowCount ?? 0} lapsed`);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * RevenueCat / Play Billing webhook. Must be authenticated — an unauthenticated\n   * endpoint here would let anyone grant themselves a paid plan.\n   */\n  async applyWebhook(event: WebhookEvent, secret?: string): Promise<{ applied: boolean; status: SubscriptionStatus }> {\n    const expected = process.env.REVENUECAT_WEBHOOK_SECRET;\n    if (!expected || secret !== expected) throw new UnauthorizedException('invalid_webhook_secret');\n\n    const user = await this.db.one<{ id: string }>('SELECT id FROM users WHERE id = $1', [\n      event.app_user_id,\n    ]);\n    if (!user) return { applied: false, status: 'inactive' };\n\n    await this.ensure(user.id);\n    const plan = PLAN_BY_PRODUCT[event.product_id ?? ''] ?? 'free';\n    const status = this.statusForEvent(event.type);\n    const renewsAt = event.expiration_at_ms ? new Date(event.expiration_at_ms) : null;\n\n    await this.db.query(\n      `UPDATE subscriptions\n          SET status = $3,\n              plan = $4,\n              daily_allowance = $5,\n              -- Upgrade grants the new allowance immediately; a lapse keeps the\n              -- remaining count untouched because the quota gate is the status.\n              likes_remaining_today = CASE WHEN $3 = 'active' THEN $5\n                                           ELSE likes_remaining_today END,\n              started_at = COALESCE(started_at, now()),\n              renews_at = $6\n        WHERE user_id = $1 AND $2`,\n      [user.id, true, status, plan, ALLOWANCE_BY_PLAN[plan] ?? ALLOWANCE_BY_PLAN.free, renewsAt],\n    );\n\n    return { applied: true, status };\n  }\n\n  private statusForEvent(type: string): SubscriptionStatus {\n    switch (type) {\n      case 'INITIAL_PURCHASE':\n      case 'RENEWAL':\n      case 'PRODUCT_CHANGE':\n      case 'UNCANCELLATION':\n      case 'TEMPORARY_ENTITLEMENT_GRANT':\n        return 'active';\n      case 'CANCELLATION':\n        // Cancelled but paid-through: access continues until renews_at passes.\n        return 'cancelled';\n      case 'EXPIRATION':\n      case 'BILLING_ISSUE':\n        return 'expired';\n      default:\n        return 'inactive';\n    }\n  }\n}\n"}

{"Write":"A:/muzz/backend/src/subscription/subscription.controller.ts: import { BadRequestException, Body, Controller, Get, Headers, Post } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { Auth, Principal, Public } from '../auth/auth';\nimport { UsersService } from '../users/users.service';\nimport { SubscriptionService, WebhookEvent } from './subscription.service';\n\n@Controller('subscription')\nexport class SubscriptionController {\n  constructor(\n    private readonly subscriptions: SubscriptionService,\n    private readonly users: UsersService,\n  ) {}\n\n  @Get('status')\n  async status(@Auth() principal: Principal) {\n    const user = await this.users.require(principal.uid);\n    return this.subscriptions.status(user.id);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * @Public because the caller is RevenueCat, not a signed-in user — the shared\n   * secret checked in applyWebhook is what authenticates it.\n   *\n   * The body is a plain interface rather than a DTO class on purpose: the global\n   * ValidationPipe runs with whitelist:true, which would strip every property of\n   * a class carrying no validation decorators, leaving an empty event. An env allowlist rather than a users.is_admin column —\n * a schema change and a migration for a list that only changes at deploy time\n * is not worth it. Move to a role table when there is more than one kind of admin.\n */\n@Injectable()\nexport class AdminGuard implements CanActivate {\n  constructor(\n    private readonly users: UsersService,\n  ) {}\n\n  async canActivate(ctx: ExecutionContext): Promise<boolean> {\n    const allowed = (process.env.ADMIN_USER_IDS ?? 'unknown';\n      final url = await Storage.upload(Storage.pathFor(userId, 'selfies'), File(shot.path));\n\n      await ref.read(verificationProvider.notifier).submit(\n            livenessSessionId: _sessionId,\n            selfieUrl: url,\n          );\n\n      final result = ref.read(verificationProvider).valueOrNull;\n      if (!mounted) return;\n      if (result?.isVerified ?? false) {\n        widget.onDone();\n      } else {\n        setState(() => _error = 'We could not verify that photo. Make sure your face is well lit and clearly visible.');\n      }\n    } catch (e) {\n      if (mounted) setState(() => _error = messageFor(e));\n    } finally {\n      if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);\n    }\n  }\n\n  @override\n  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n    final camera = _camera;\n\n    return Scaffold(\n      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Verify it’s you')),\n      body: Column(\n        children: [\n          Expanded(\n            child: ColoredBox(\n              color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surfaceContainerHighest,\n              child: camera == null || !camera.value.isInitialized\n                  ? const LoadingView()\n                  : Semantics(\n                      image: true,\n                      label: 'Live camera preview',\n                      child: Center(child: CameraPreview(camera)),\n                    ),\n            ),\n          ),\n          Padding(\n            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Tokens.spaceMd),\n            child: Column(\n              children: [\n                Text(\n                  'Take a live selfie. We compare it with your main photo — this is what keeps Rishta free of fake profiles.',\n                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,\n                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,\n                ),\n                const SizedBox(height: Tokens.spaceMd),\n                if (_error != null) ...[\n                  Semantics(\n                    liveRegion: true,\n                    child: Text(\n                      _error!,\n                      textAlign: TextAlign.center,\n                      style: TextStyle(color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.error),\n                    ),\n                  ),\n                  const SizedBox(height: Tokens.spaceSm),\n                ],\n                FilledButton.icon(\n                  onPressed: _busy || camera == null ? null : _capture,\n                  icon: const Icon(Icons.camera_alt),\n                  label: Text(_busy ? Icons.error_outline : Icons.hourglass_top,\n                  size: 48,\n                  semanticLabel: value == VerificationStatus.failed ? 'Verification failed' : 'Verification pending',\n                ),\n                const SizedBox(height: Tokens.spaceMd),\n                Text(\n                  value == VerificationStatus.failed\n                      ? {"Write":"A:/muzz/README.md: # Rishta\n\nFlutter + NestJS/PostgreSQL matchmaking app, built from [prompt.md](prompt.md).\n\n```\nbackend/   NestJS API, PostgreSQL schema\napp/       Flutter client (Riverpod)\n```\n\n---\n\n## Backend\n\n```bash\ncd backend\nnpm install\ncp .env.example .env          # fill in DATABASE_URL at minimum\nnpm run db:init               # applies sql/schema.sql then sql/seed.sql\nnpm run dev                   # nest start --watch on :3000\nnpm run typecheck             # tsc --noEmit\n```\n\n`GET /auth/health` is public and confirms the process is up without needing\nFirebase credentials.\n\n### Where the critical rules live\n\nSpec §2.3 lists the rules that must not be bypassable. Most are DB-level, so a\nfuture code path that forgets a rule still cannot break it:\n\n| Rule | Enforced by |\n|---|---|\n| Minimum age 18 | `users_min_age` trigger — raises on any insert/update of `dob` |\n| Verified phone unique | partial unique index `users_phone_verified_uniq` |\n| Re-verify on primary photo change | `profiles_reverify_on_photo_change` trigger |\n| No duplicate matches | normalized pair + `CHECK (user_a_id < user_b_id)` + unique index |\n| Atomic like quota | application SQL — a live counter, so it stays a single `UPDATE ... WHERE likes_remaining_today > 0` with a rows-affected check |\n| Quota resets nightly, never on login | `@Cron` in `SubscriptionService.nightlyQuotaReset` |\n| Lapse gates new likes only | `FeedService.like` requires `status = 'active'`; `/matches` and chat read no subscription state at all |\n| Feed excludes passes and blocks | `FeedService.feed` — `NOT EXISTS` on both tables, in both directions |\n\n### Notable implementations\n\n- **`Repo<T>`** (`src/db/db.ts`) — one generic table gateway for every table.\n  Table and column names come from server-authored code, never a request body.\n- **Stream tokens** (`src/auth/stream-token.ts`) — a 10-line HS256 JWT via\n  `node:crypto` instead of pulling in `stream-chat`'s server SDK for one token.\n- **Moderation** (`src/moderation/`) — flags for human review rather than\n  hard-blocking, per §2.5. Both providers degrade to passthrough when their\n  credentials are absent, so the pipeline is exercisable in development. In\n  production an unconfigured verification provider refuses rather than\n  auto-approving (`VerificationService.assertProvider`).\n\n---\n\n## App\n\n```bash\ncd app\nflutter pub get\nflutter run --dart-define=API_BASE=http://10.0.2.2:3000 \\\n            --dart-define=STREAM_API_KEY=your_stream_key\n```\n\nRequires `google-services.json` (Android) / `GoogleService-Info.plist` (iOS) for\nFirebase. No generated `firebase_options.dart` is needed unless you target web\nor macOS as well.\n\n`10.0.2.2` is the Android emulator's alias for the host machine.\n\n### Structure\n\n- `lib/core/` — `theme.dart` (design tokens — the layer that mirrors to Figma),\n  `api.dart` (one generic request path), `models.dart`, `providers.dart`,\n  `widgets.dart` (`AsyncView` collapses loading/error/empty/data for every screen)\n- `lib/features/` — one folder per area, one file per screen\n\n---\n\n## Deliberate deviations from the spec\n\n1. **`profiles.name` added.** §1.2 lists Name as a profile-creation field; the\n   §2.2 data model has no column for it. The endpoint would otherwise be unable\n   to store a required field.\n2. **`notification_preferences`, `device_tokens`, `moderation_flags` added.**\n   §2.4 and §2.5 require the endpoints and the flagging pipeline, but §2.2 has\n   no tables for them.\n3. **`gender` and `dob` are nullable.** `POST /auth/verify-token` creates the\n   users row before onboarding runs, so a `NOT NULL dob` would reject signup.\n   A paired `CHECK` keeps the two consistent, and the age trigger still fires\n   the moment `dob` is set.\n4. **The feed also excludes already-liked profiles.** §2.3 names passes and\n   blocks only, but an outgoing like is a swipe-past too, and §1.2 says a card\n   the user already swiped should never re-render.\n5. **`CANCELLATION` keeps status `active`.** A cancelled subscription has been\n   paid through `renews_at`; marking it `cancelled` would revoke access the user\n   already bought. The nightly job expires it once that date passes.\n6. **Photo bytes never touch the API.** The client uploads straight to Firebase\n   Storage and `POST /profile/photo` records the resulting URL.\n7. **`flutter_svg` omitted** — the spec lists it under UI polish, but the app has\n   no SVG assets. An unused dependency is worse than a missing one; add it when\n   the first vector asset lands.\n\n## Still open\n\n- **Store billing.** `SubscriptionScreen._purchase` is a clearly-marked stub —\n  RevenueCat/Play Billing is the remaining piece of build step 7. The server\n  side it feeds (`POST /subscription/webhook`) is complete and authenticated.\n- **Face Liveness client SDK.** The server implements the full\n  `CreateFaceLivenessSession` → `GetFaceLivenessSessionResults` → `CompareFaces`\n  flow. The app currently captures with the `camera` package and submits; swap in\n  Amplify's `FaceLivenessDetector` against the returned `sessionId` for\n  production-grade liveness.\n- **Device fingerprint.** `users.device_id` and the per-device account cap are\n  implemented; the client does not yet send one. Firebase App Check is the\n  intended source (§2.3).\n"}

{"user":"in memory folder make llm wiki for obsidian map\n# LLM Wiki\n\n\nA personal knowledge base maintained by Claude Code.\nBased on Andrej Karpathy's LLM Wiki pattern.\n\n\n## Purpose\n\n\nThis wiki is a structured, interlinked knowledge base for planning a trip to Japan.\nClaude maintains the wiki. The human curates sources, asks questions, and guides the analysis.\n\n\n## Folder structure\n\n\n```\nraw/          -- source documents (immutable -- never modify these)\nwiki/         -- markdown pages maintained by Claude\nwiki/index.md -- table of contents for the entire wiki\nwiki/log.md   -- append-only record of all operations\n```\n\n\n## Ingest workflow\n\n\nWhen the user adds a new source to `raw/` and asks you to ingest it:\n\n\n1. Read the full source document\n2. Discuss key takeaways with the user before writing anything\n3. Create a summary page in `wiki/` named after the source\n4. Create or update concept pages for each major idea or entity\n5. Add wiki-links ([[page-name]]) to connect related pages\n6. Update `wiki/index.md` with new pages and one-line descriptions\n7. Append an entry to `wiki/log.md` with the date, source name, and what changed\n\n\nA single source may touch 10-15 wiki pages. That is normal.\n\n\n## Page format\n\n\nEvery wiki page should follow this structure:\n\n\n```markdown\n# Page Title\n\n\n**Summary**: One to two sentences describing this page.\n\n\n**Sources**: List of raw source files this page draws from.\n\n\n**Last updated**: Date of most recent update.\n\n\n---\n\n\nMain content goes here. Use clear headings and short paragraphs.\n\n\nLink to related concepts using [[wiki-links]] throughout the text.\n\n\n## Related pages\n\n\n- [[related-concept-1]]\n- [[related-concept-2]]\n```\n\n\n## Citation rules\n\n\n- Every factual claim should reference its source file\n- Use the format (source: filename.pdf) after the claim\n- If two sources disagree, note the contradiction explicitly\n- If a claim has no source, mark it as needing verification\n\n\n## Question answering\n\n\nWhen the user asks a question:\n\n\n1. Read `wiki/index.md` first to find relevant pages\n2. Read those pages and synthesize an answer\n3. Cite specific wiki pages in your response\n4. If the answer is not in the wiki, say so clearly\n5. If the answer is valuable, offer to save it as a new wiki page\n\n\nGood answers should be filed back into the wiki so they compound over time.\n\n\n## Lint\n\n\nWhen the user asks you to lint or audit the wiki:\n\n\n- Check for contradictions between pages\n- Find orphan pages (no inbound links from other pages)\n- Identify concepts mentioned in pages that lack their own page\n- Flag claims that may be outdated based on newer sources\n- Check that all pages follow the page format above\n- Report findings as a numbered list with suggested fixes\n\n\n## Rules\n\n\n- Never modify anything in the `raw/` folder\n- Always update `wiki/index.md` and `wiki/log.md` after changes\n- Keep page names lowercase with hyphens (e.g. `machine-learning.md`)\n- Write in clear, plain language\n- When uncertain about how to categorize something, ask the user"}

{"Write":"A:/muzz/memory/raw/README.md: # LLM Wiki\n\nA personal knowledge base maintained by Claude Code. Based on Andrej Karpathy's LLM Wiki pattern.\n\n## Purpose\n\nThis wiki is a structured, interlinked knowledge base for the **Rishta** matchmaking project (Flutter frontend + NestJS/PostgreSQL backend).\nClaude maintains the wiki. The human curates sources, asks questions, and guides the analysis.\n\n## Folder structure\n\n```\nraw/          -- source documents (immutable -- never modify these)\nwiki/         -- markdown pages maintained by Claude\nwiki/index.md -- table of contents for the entire wiki\nwiki/log.md   -- append-only record of all operations\n```\n\n## Ingest workflow\n\nWhen the user adds a new source to `raw/` and asks to ingest it:\n\n1. Read the full source document\n2. Discuss key takeaways with the user before writing anything\n3. Create a summary page in `wiki/` named after the source\n4. Create or update concept pages for each major idea or entity\n5. Add wiki-links (`[[page-name]]`) to connect related pages\n6. Update `wiki/index.md` with new pages and one-line descriptions\n7. Append an entry to `wiki/log.md` with the date, source name, and what changed\n\nA single source may touch 10-15 wiki pages. That is normal.\n\n## Page format\n\nEvery wiki page uses:\n\n```markdown\n# Page Title\n\n**Summary**: One to two sentences describing this page.\n\n**Sources**: List of raw source files this page draws from.\n\n**Last updated**: Date of most recent update.\n\n---\n\nMain content here. Link to related concepts using [[wiki-links]].\n\n## Related pages\n\n- [[related-concept-1]]\n```\n\n## Citation rules\n\n- Every factual claim references its source file: `(source: filename.md)`\n- Note contradictions explicitly when two sources disagree\n- Mark unsourced claims *needing verification*\n\n## Question answering\n\n1. Read `wiki/index.md` first to find relevant pages\n2. Read those pages and synthesize an answer\n3. Cite specific wiki pages in the response\n4. Say so clearly if the answer is not in the wiki\n5. Offer to file valuable answers back into the wiki\n\n## Lint\n\nWhen asked to lint the wiki: check contradictions, orphan pages, concepts missing pages, outdated claims, and format compliance. Report as a numbered list with fixes.\n\n## Rules\n\n- Never modify anything in `raw/`\n- Always update `wiki/index.md` and `wiki/log.md` after changes\n- Keep page names lowercase with hyphens (e.g. `quota-design.md`)\n- Write in clear, plain language"}

One line per page. -->"}

{"Write":"A:/muzz/backend/db-init.js: // db-init.js — applies sql/*.sql to the database named in DATABASE_URL.\n//\n// Replaces the old `psql \"$DATABASE_URL\"` script: that required the PostgreSQL\n// client tools on PATH AND $DATABASE_URL to be expanded by the shell. On Windows\n// npm runs scripts through cmd, which expands neither. This uses the already-\n// installed `pg` dependency instead, so no client tools and no shell quoting.\nconst fs = require('fs');\nconst path = require('path');\n\n// Load .env manually. dotenv exists only as a transitive dep of @nestjs/config,\n// so a 5-line parser keeps this script self-contained instead of depending on a\n// package that is not in our own package.json.\nconst envPath = path.join(__dirname, '.env');\nif (fs.existsSync(envPath)) {\n  for (const line of fs.readFileSync(envPath, 'utf8').split(/\\r?\\n/)) {\n    const m = line.match(/^\\s*([A-Z0-9_]+)\\s*=\\s*(.*?)\\s*$/i);\n    if (m && m[1] && m[2]) process.env[m[1]] = m[2];\n  }\n}\n\nconst { Client } = require('pg');\n\nasync function main() {\n  const url = process.env.DATABASE_URL;\n  if (!url) throw new Error('DATABASE_URL not found — expected it in backend/.env');\n\n  const client = new Client({ connectionString: url });\n  await client.connect();\n  const base = __dirname;\n  const schema = fs.readFileSync(path.join(base, 'sql', 'schema.sql'), 'utf8');\n  const seed = fs.readFileSync(path.join(base, 'sql', 'seed.sql'), 'utf8');\n\n  console.log(`Connected. Applying schema.sql (${schema.length} bytes) …`);\n  await client.query(schema);\n  console.log('schema.sql applied.');\n\n  console.log(`Applying seed.sql (${seed.length} bytes) …`);\n  await client.query(seed);\n  console.log('seed.sql applied. Done.');\n\n  await client.end();\n}\n\nmain().catch((err) => {\n  const detail = err && err.message;\n  console.error('db:init failed:', detail);\n  if (typeof detail === 'string' && /does not exist/i.test(detail) && /database/i.test(detail)) {\n    console.error('Hint: the target database does not exist yet. Create it first, e.g.:');\n    console.error('  \"C:/Program Files/PostgreSQL/17/bin/createdb\" -U postgres rishta');\n  }\n  process.exit(1);\n});"}
